API

Payment

Operations

Every route this entity exposes. Each route links to its own reference on this page.

Method Route SDK operation Returns
POST /api/v1/paymentrequests/{id}/directdebit create No result is returned
POST /api/v1/paymentrequests create OK
GET /api/v1/paymentrequests/{id} load Returns a payment request record
GET /api/v1/paymentrequests/getbyorderid/{orderID} load Returns a payment request record
PUT /api/v1/paymentrequests/{id} update Returns the updated payment request record

Fields

Field Type Required Description
addresses array No
amount number No The amount of money to request.
amountPending number No Total amount that has been authorised but not settled for this payment request.
amountReceived number No Total amount received for this payment request.
amountRefunded number No Total amount refunded for this payment request.
autoSendReceipt boolean No If set to true, a receipt will be automatically sent to the CustomerEmailAddress when payments are received.
baseOriginUrl string No For card payments the origin of the payment page needs to be set in advance.

create

POST /api/v1/paymentrequests/{id}/directdebit

Operation ID: SubmitDirectDebitForMandate.

Authentication: API key in the header (Bearer).

Parameters

| Parameter | In | Type | Required | Description | | --- | --- | --- | --- | | id | path | string (uuid) | Yes | ID of the payment request to pay. | | mandateID | query | string (uuid) | No | ID of the existing Direct Debit mandate record. | | submitAfter | query | string (date-time) | No | Defines when this payment should be earliest submitted to the customer's bank account. Optional. Payments will be submitted as soon as possible, if not specified. |

Responses

200: No result is returned
400: Configuration error
404: Payment request or mandate were not found

POST /api/v1/paymentrequests

Operation ID: CreatePaymentRequest.

Authentication: API key in the header (Bearer).

Request body

application/json

Property Type Required Description
amount number (double) Yes The amount of money to request.
autoSendReceipt boolean No If set to true, a receipt will be automatically sent to the CustomerEmailAddress when payments are received.
callbackUrl string No Once a payment is processed, or a notification of an inbound payment is received, a callback request will be made to this URL. Typically it will be the page on a merchant's web site that displays the results of the payment attempt.
cardAuthorizeOnly boolean No For card payments the default behaviour is to authorise and capture the payment at the same time. If a merchant needs to authorise and then capture at a later point this property needs to be set to true.
cardCreateToken boolean No For card payments a payment attempt can be used to create a reusable token for subsequent payments. Setting this field to true will create a reusable customer token.
cardCreateTokenMode enum: None, ConsentNotRequired, UserConsentRequired No This specifies whether user consent will be taken before tokenising card or not. This cannot be 'None' if CardCreateToken is set to true. If this is set to 'UserConsentRequired' then, the user consent will overwrite CardCreateToken flag on submit card payment.
cardIgnoreCVN boolean No If set to true the card payment gateway will be directed to proceed with a payment even if the card verification number check fails.
cardNoPayerAuthentication boolean No If set to true for card payments no attempt will be made to use payer authentication (3-D Secure and equivalent). Skipping payer authentication can help avoid failed payment attempts when a payer is not enrolled or when they can't be bothered completing their issuing bank's authentication steps. A disadvantage is it exposes the merchant to liability for charge backs.
cardProcessorMerchantID string No Optional field that if specified indicates the processor merchant ID that should be used to process any card payments. Mainly useful where a merchant has multiple processor merchant ID's. If left empty the default merchant card settings will be used.
cardTransmitRawDetails boolean No If set to true for card payments the sensitive card number and card verification number will be transmitted directly rather than being tokenised. This makes the payment quicker but more exposed to client side flaws such as cross site scripting.
currency enum: NONE, GBP, EUR, USD, AUD, BGN, ... No The currency of the payment request.
customFields array of object No A list of custom fields to add to the payment request. The custom fields are data type agnostic which means that the API will not do any validation or formatting in the key-value pairs. The API will store the custom fields as is.
customFields[].displayOnHostedPaymentPage boolean No If true, the custom field will be displayed on the hosted payment page.
customFields[].displayOnPaymentReceipt boolean No If true, the custom field will be displayed on the payment receipt.
customFields[].displayOrder integer (int32) No The display order of the custom field. The lowest number is displayed first. This is used to determine the order in which the custom fields are displayed on the UI, for example, on the hosted payment page and payment receipt.
customFields[].name string Yes The name of the custom field.
customFields[].value string Yes The value of the custom field.
customerEmailAddress string No Optional email address for the customer. If the tokenise card option is set then the customer email address is mandatory.
customerID string No An optional customer identifier for the payment request. This field is sent to the payer's bank when using payment initiation. The restriction in the available characters is due to some banks rejecting requests when ones outside the set are used.
description string No An optional description for the payment request. If set this field will appear on the transaction record for some card processors.
dueDate string (date-time) No An optional due date for the payment request.
failureCallbackUrl string No Optional callback URL for payment failures that can occur when the payer is redirected away from the payment page. Typically the payer is only sent away from the payment page for pay by bank attempts. If this URL is not set the payer will be redirected back to the original URL the payment attempt was initiated from.
fieldDisplaySettings array of object No An optional display settings for the payment request fields. This allows the merchant to control how the fields are displayed on the hosted payment page, receipt, etc.
fieldDisplaySettings[].displayOnHostedPaymentPage boolean No If false, the field will not be displayed on the hosted payment page.
fieldDisplaySettings[].displayOnPaymentReceipt boolean No If false, the field will not be displayed on the payment receipt.
fieldDisplaySettings[].field enum: None, Description, Customer, DueDate No The field for which the display settings are defined.
ignoreAddressVerification boolean No If set to true the card payment gateway will be directed to proceed with a payment even if the address verification checks fails.
merchantDirectDebitMandateID string (uuid) No Optional ID of an existing direct debit mandate to associate with this payment request. Only applicable when the payment method is direct debit.
merchantID string (uuid) No The ID of the merchant to create the payment request for.
notificationEmailAddresses string (email) No
notificationRoleIDs array of string (uuid) No An optional list of notification role IDs that will receive notifications about the payment request. This is useful for roles that need to be notified about payment request events.
orderID string No An optional order ID for the payment request. If the request is for an invoice this is the most appropriate field for the invoice ID.
partialPaymentMethod enum: None, Partial No The approach to use, or not, for accepting partial payments.
partialPaymentSteps string No An optional comma separated list of partial payment amounts. The amounts represent guidance, or suggestions, as to how the payer will be requested to make partial payments.
paymentMethods array of enum: None, card, pisp, lightning, cardtoken, applePay, ... No The payment methods that the payment request supports.
payrunID string (uuid) No Optional, if set it indicates that this payment request will be used to top up a payment account for a pay run.
pispAccountID string (uuid) No The payment account ID to use to receive payment initiation payments. This must match one of your NoFrixion payment account IDs. This can be left blank to use your default payment account.
priorityBankID string (uuid) No The ID of the bank that is set as the priority bank for display on pay element.
sandboxSettleDelayInSeconds integer (int32) No Sandbox only. Optional. If set, the simulated Direct Debit settlement will be delayed by the specified number of seconds. Must be greater than 0 and less than 600. Otherwise, the default value will be used.
shippingAddressCity string No Optionally the city of the customer's shipping address.
shippingAddressCountryCode string No Optionally the country code of the customer's shipping address.
shippingAddressCounty string No Optionally the state or county of the customer's shipping address.
shippingAddressLine1 string No Optionally the first line of the customer's shipping address.
shippingAddressLine2 string No Optionally the second line of the customer's shipping address.
shippingAddressPostCode string No Optionally the post code of the customer's shipping address.
shippingEmail string No Optionally the shipping email address for the customer.
shippingFirstName string No Optionally the first name of the customer's shipping address.
shippingLastName string No Optionally the last name of the customer's shipping address.
shippingPhone string No Optionally the shipping phone number for the customer.
successWebHookUrl string No If a payment event results in the payment request being classified as fully paid this success webhook URL will be invoked. The URL will be invoked as a GET request, that is there will be no request body. Two query parameters will be added to the URL. The first one will be "id" and will hold the payment request ID. The second one will be "orderid" and will hold the payment request OrderID, note the OrderID could be empty if it was not set when the payment request was created. The recommended approach when receiving a success web hook is to use the "id" parameter to call the moneymoov get payment request endpoint to retrieve the full details of the payment request and check the status. Web hooks can be easily spoofed and should not be relied upon.
tagIds array of string (uuid) No An optional list of tag ids to add to the payment request
tags array of string No An optional list of tag values to set on the payment request. If no matching tag exists it will be created.
title string No A generic field to contain any additional data that the merchant wishes to store against the payment request. for example product or service information.
useHostedPaymentPage boolean No If set to true, and the merchant is configured for hosted payment pages, the base and callback URLs will be set to use the hosted payment page.

Responses

200: OK

successWebHookUrl string No If a payment event results in the payment request being classified as fully paid this success webhook URL will be invoked. The URL will be invoked as a GET request, that is there will be no request body. Two query parameters will be added to the URL. The first one will be "id" and will hold the payment request ID. The second one will be "orderid" and will hold the payment request OrderID, note the OrderID could be empty if it was not set when the payment request was created. The recommended approach when receiving a success web hook is to use the "id" parameter to call the moneymoov get payment request endpoint to retrieve the full details of the payment request and check the status. Web hooks can be easily spoofed and should not be relied upon.

status enum: None, FullyPaid, PartiallyPaid, OverPaid, Voided, Authorized No The current status of the payment request. Will be set to FullyPaid when the full amount has been received.
successWebHookUrl string No If a payment event results in the payment request being classified as fully paid this success webhook URL will be invoked. The URL will be invoked as a GET request, that is there will be no request body. Two query parameters will be added to the URL. The first one will be "id" and will hold the payment request ID. The second one will be "orderid" and will hold the payment request OrderID, note the OrderID could be empty if it was not set when the payment request was created. The recommended approach when receiving a success web hook is to use the "id" parameter to call the moneymoov get payment request endpoint to retrieve the full details of the payment request and check the status. Web hooks can be easily spoofed and should not be relied upon.

status enum: None, FullyPaid, PartiallyPaid, OverPaid, Voided, Authorized No The current status of the payment request. Will be set to FullyPaid when the full amount has been received.
successWebHookUrl string No If a payment event results in the payment request being classified as fully paid this success webhook URL will be invoked. The URL will be invoked as a GET request, that is there will be no request body. Two query parameters will be added to the URL. The first one will be "id" and will hold the payment request ID. The second one will be "orderid" and will hold the payment request OrderID, note the OrderID could be empty if it was not set when the payment request was created. The recommended approach when receiving a success web hook is to use the "id" parameter to call the moneymoov get payment request endpoint to retrieve the full details of the payment request and check the status. Web hooks can be easily spoofed and should not be relied upon.

useHostedPaymentPage boolean No If set to true, and the merchant is configured for hosted payment pages, the base and callback URLs will be set to use the hosted payment page.
201: Returns the newly created payment request record

load

GET /api/v1/paymentrequests/{id}

Operation ID: GetPaymentRequest.

Authentication: API key in the header (Bearer).

Parameters

| Parameter | In | Type | Required | Description | | --- | --- | --- | --- | | id | path | string (uuid) | Yes | The ID of the payment request to retrieve. | | includeEvents | query | boolean | No | Set to true to include any associated payment request events. |

Responses

200: Returns a payment request record

successWebHookUrl string No If a payment event results in the payment request being classified as fully paid this success webhook URL will be invoked. The URL will be invoked as a GET request, that is there will be no request body. Two query parameters will be added to the URL. The first one will be "id" and will hold the payment request ID. The second one will be "orderid" and will hold the payment request OrderID, note the OrderID could be empty if it was not set when the payment request was created. The recommended approach when receiving a success web hook is to use the "id" parameter to call the moneymoov get payment request endpoint to retrieve the full details of the payment request and check the status. Web hooks can be easily spoofed and should not be relied upon.

status enum: None, FullyPaid, PartiallyPaid, OverPaid, Voided, Authorized No The current status of the payment request. Will be set to FullyPaid when the full amount has been received.
successWebHookUrl string No If a payment event results in the payment request being classified as fully paid this success webhook URL will be invoked. The URL will be invoked as a GET request, that is there will be no request body. Two query parameters will be added to the URL. The first one will be "id" and will hold the payment request ID. The second one will be "orderid" and will hold the payment request OrderID, note the OrderID could be empty if it was not set when the payment request was created. The recommended approach when receiving a success web hook is to use the "id" parameter to call the moneymoov get payment request endpoint to retrieve the full details of the payment request and check the status. Web hooks can be easily spoofed and should not be relied upon.

status enum: None, FullyPaid, PartiallyPaid, OverPaid, Voided, Authorized No The current status of the payment request. Will be set to FullyPaid when the full amount has been received.
successWebHookUrl string No If a payment event results in the payment request being classified as fully paid this success webhook URL will be invoked. The URL will be invoked as a GET request, that is there will be no request body. Two query parameters will be added to the URL. The first one will be "id" and will hold the payment request ID. The second one will be "orderid" and will hold the payment request OrderID, note the OrderID could be empty if it was not set when the payment request was created. The recommended approach when receiving a success web hook is to use the "id" parameter to call the moneymoov get payment request endpoint to retrieve the full details of the payment request and check the status. Web hooks can be easily spoofed and should not be relied upon.

GET /api/v1/paymentrequests/getbyorderid/{orderID}

Operation ID: GetPaymentRequestForOrder.

Authentication: API key in the header (Bearer).

Parameters

| Parameter | In | Type | Required | Description | | --- | --- | --- | --- | | orderID | path | string | Yes | The order ID of the payment request to retrieve. Useful when the payment request has been created by an external application such as WooCommerce or Xero. |

Responses

200: Returns a payment request record

successWebHookUrl string No If a payment event results in the payment request being classified as fully paid this success webhook URL will be invoked. The URL will be invoked as a GET request, that is there will be no request body. Two query parameters will be added to the URL. The first one will be "id" and will hold the payment request ID. The second one will be "orderid" and will hold the payment request OrderID, note the OrderID could be empty if it was not set when the payment request was created. The recommended approach when receiving a success web hook is to use the "id" parameter to call the moneymoov get payment request endpoint to retrieve the full details of the payment request and check the status. Web hooks can be easily spoofed and should not be relied upon.

status enum: None, FullyPaid, PartiallyPaid, OverPaid, Voided, Authorized No The current status of the payment request. Will be set to FullyPaid when the full amount has been received.
successWebHookUrl string No If a payment event results in the payment request being classified as fully paid this success webhook URL will be invoked. The URL will be invoked as a GET request, that is there will be no request body. Two query parameters will be added to the URL. The first one will be "id" and will hold the payment request ID. The second one will be "orderid" and will hold the payment request OrderID, note the OrderID could be empty if it was not set when the payment request was created. The recommended approach when receiving a success web hook is to use the "id" parameter to call the moneymoov get payment request endpoint to retrieve the full details of the payment request and check the status. Web hooks can be easily spoofed and should not be relied upon.

successWebHookUrl string No If a payment event results in the payment request being classified as fully paid this success webhook URL will be invoked. The URL will be invoked as a GET request, that is there will be no request body. Two query parameters will be added to the URL. The first one will be "id" and will hold the payment request ID. The second one will be "orderid" and will hold the payment request OrderID, note the OrderID could be empty if it was not set when the payment request was created. The recommended approach when receiving a success web hook is to use the "id" parameter to call the moneymoov get payment request endpoint to retrieve the full details of the payment request and check the status. Web hooks can be easily spoofed and should not be relied upon.

useHostedPaymentPage boolean No If set to true, and the merchant is configured for hosted payment pages, the base and callback URLs will be set to use the hosted payment page.

update

PUT /api/v1/paymentrequests/{id}

Operation ID: UpdatePaymentRequest.

Authentication: API key in the header (Bearer).

Parameters

| Parameter | In | Type | Required | Description | | --- | --- | --- | --- | | id | path | string (uuid) | Yes | The ID of the payment request to update. |

Request body

application/json

Property Type Required Description
amount number (double) No
autoSendReceipt boolean No If set to true, a receipt will be automatically sent to the CustomerEmailAddress when payments are received.
baseOriginUrl string No For card payments the origin of the payment page needs to be set in advance. A public key context is generated to encrypt sensitive card details and is bound to a single origin URL.
callbackUrl string No Once a payment is processed, or a notification of an inbound payment is received, a callback request will be made to this URL. Typically it will be the page on a merchant's web site that displays the results of the payment attempt.
cardAuthorizeOnly boolean No For card payments the default behaviour is to authorise and capture the payment at the same time. If a merchant needs to authorise and then capture at a later point this property needs to be set to true.
cardCreateToken boolean No For card payments a payment attempt can be used to create a reusable token for subsequent payments. Setting this field to true will create a reusable customer token.
cardCreateTokenMode enum: None, ConsentNotRequired, UserConsentRequired No This specifies whether user consent will be taken before tokenising card or not. This cannot be 'None' if CardCreateToken is set to true. If this is set to 'UserConsentRequired' then, the user consent will overwrite CardCreateToken flag on submit card payment.
cardIgnoreCVN boolean No
cardProcessorMerchantID string No Optional field that if specified indicates the processor merchant ID that should be used to process any card payments. Mainly useful where a merchant has multiple processor merchant ID's. If left empty the default merchant card settings will be used.
currency enum: NONE, GBP, EUR, USD, AUD, BGN, ... No
customFields array of object No A list of custom fields to add to the payment request. The custom fields are data type agnostic which means that the API will not do any validation or formatting in the key-value pairs. The API will store the custom fields as is.
customFields[].displayOnHostedPaymentPage boolean No If true, the custom field will be displayed on the hosted payment page.
customFields[].displayOnPaymentReceipt boolean No If true, the custom field will be displayed on the payment receipt.
customFields[].displayOrder integer (int32) No The display order of the custom field. The lowest number is displayed first. This is used to determine the order in which the custom fields are displayed on the UI, for example, on the hosted payment page and payment receipt.
customFields[].name string Yes The name of the custom field.
customFields[].value string Yes The value of the custom field.
customerEmailAddress string (email) No Optional email address for the customer. If the tokenise card option is set then the customer email address is mandatory.
customerID string No An optional customer identifier for the payment request. This field is sent to the payer's bank when using payment initiation. The restriction in the available characters is due to some banks rejecting requests when ones outside the set are used.
description string No An optional description for the payment request. If set this field will appear on the transaction record for some card processors.
dueDate string (date-time) No The due date of the payment request.
failureCallbackUrl string No Optional callback URL for payment failures that can occur when the payer is redirected away from the payment page. Typically the payer is only sent away from the payment page for pay by bank attempts. If this URL is not set the payer will be redirected back to the original URL the payment attempt was initiated from.
ignoreAddressVerification boolean No
lightningInvoice string No Bitcoin Lightning invoice for the payment request.
lightningInvoiceExpiresAt string (date-time) No Date and time of expiration of the lightning invoice.
notificationEmailAddresses string (email) No
orderID string No An optional order ID for the payment request. If the request is for an invoice this is the most appropriate field for the invoice ID.
partialPaymentSteps string No An optional comma separated list of partial payment amounts. The amounts represent guidance, or suggestions, as to how the payer will be requested to make partial payments.
paymentMethods array of enum: None, card, pisp, lightning, cardtoken, applePay, ... No The payment methods that the payment request supports.
pispAccountID string (uuid) No The payment account ID to use to receive payment initiation payments. This must match one of your NoFrixion payment account IDs. This can be left blank to use your default payment account.
shippingAddressCity string No Optionally the city of the customer's shipping address.
shippingAddressCountryCode string No Optionally the country code of the customer's shipping address.
shippingAddressCounty string No Optionally the state or county of the customer's shipping address.
shippingAddressLine1 string No Optionally the first line of the customer's shipping address.
shippingAddressLine2 string No Optionally the second line of the customer's shipping address.
shippingAddressPostCode string No Optionally the post code of the customer's shipping address.
shippingEmail string (email) No Optionally the shipping email address for the customer.
shippingFirstName string No Optionally the first name of the customer's shipping address.
shippingLastName string No Optionally the last name of the customer's shipping address.
shippingPhone string No Optionally the shipping phone number for the customer.
successWebHookUrl string No If a payment event results in the payment request being classified as fully paid this success webhook URL will be invoked. The URL will be invoked as a GET request, that is there will be no request body. Two query parameters will be added to the URL. The first one will be "id" and will hold the payment request ID. The second one will be "orderid" and will hold the payment request OrderID, note the OrderID could be empty if it was not set when the payment request was created. The recommended approach when receiving a success web hook is to use the "id" parameter to call the moneymoov get payment request endpoint to retrieve the full details of the payment request and check the status. Web hooks can be easily spoofed and should not be relied upon.
tagIds array of string (uuid) No An optional list of tag ids to add to the payment request
title string No A generic field to contain any additional data that the merchant wishes to store against the payment request. for example product or service information.

status enum: None, FullyPaid, PartiallyPaid, OverPaid, Voided, Authorized No The current status of the payment request. Will be set to FullyPaid when the full amount has been received.
successWebHookUrl string No If a payment event results in the payment request being classified as fully paid this success webhook URL will be invoked. The URL will be invoked as a GET request, that is there will be no request body. Two query parameters will be added to the URL. The first one will be "id" and will hold the payment request ID. The second one will be "orderid" and will hold the payment request OrderID, note the OrderID could be empty if it was not set when the payment request was created. The recommended approach when receiving a success web hook is to use the "id" parameter to call the moneymoov get payment request endpoint to retrieve the full details of the payment request and check the status. Web hooks can be easily spoofed and should not be relied upon.

status enum: None, FullyPaid, PartiallyPaid, OverPaid, Voided, Authorized No The current status of the payment request. Will be set to FullyPaid when the full amount has been received.
successWebHookUrl string No If a payment event results in the payment request being classified as fully paid this success webhook URL will be invoked. The URL will be invoked as a GET request, that is there will be no request body. Two query parameters will be added to the URL. The first one will be "id" and will hold the payment request ID. The second one will be "orderid" and will hold the payment request OrderID, note the OrderID could be empty if it was not set when the payment request was created. The recommended approach when receiving a success web hook is to use the "id" parameter to call the moneymoov get payment request endpoint to retrieve the full details of the payment request and check the status. Web hooks can be easily spoofed and should not be relied upon.

Typically the payer is only sent away from the payment page for pay by bank attempts. If this URL is not set the payer will be redirected back to the original URL the payment attempt was initiated from.

status enum: None, FullyPaid, PartiallyPaid, OverPaid, Voided, Authorized No The current status of the payment request. Will be set to FullyPaid when the full amount has been received.
successWebHookUrl string No If a payment event results in the payment request being classified as fully paid this success webhook URL will be invoked. The URL will be invoked as a GET request, that is there will be no request body. Two query parameters will be added to the URL. The first one will be "id" and will hold the payment request ID. The second one will be "orderid" and will hold the payment request OrderID, note the OrderID could be empty if it was not set when the payment request was created. The recommended approach when receiving a success web hook is to use the "id" parameter to call the moneymoov get payment request endpoint to retrieve the full details of the payment request and check the status. Web hooks can be easily spoofed and should not be relied upon.
